# Chocolate reduced-fat milk

<section class="hmi-quick-read" id="chocolate-reduced-fat-milk-quick-read" data-hmi-quick-read aria-labelledby="chocolate-reduced-fat-milk-quick-read-label"><div class="hmi-quick-read-label" id="chocolate-reduced-fat-milk-quick-read-label">Quick read</div>

Chocolate reduced-fat milk in the HMI library has 68 reported results for lead, cadmium, total arsenic, total mercury, nickel, chromium, aluminium, uranium, Co, Cu, Mn, Zn, Se, Ca, F and K from 7 sources.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Burgon VH · 2023</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Cacau e Chocolates &quot;Bean to Bar&quot;: Contaminantes Inorgânicos e Ocratoxina A (Cocoa beans and &quot;Bean to Bar&quot; chocolates: Inorganic contaminants and Ochratoxin A)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Master&#39;s thesis, Instituto de Tecnologia de Alimentos (ITAL), Centro de Ciência e Qualidade de Alimentos, Campinas-SP, Brazil</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fsanz2011-23rd-australian-total-diet-study|5]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Food Standards Australia New Zealand · 2011</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fsanz2011-23rd-australian-total-diet-study">The 23rd Australian Total Diet Study</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Standards Australia New Zealand</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fsanz2011-23rd-australian-total-diet-study">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fsanz2019-25th-australian-total-diet-study|6]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Food Standards Australia New Zealand · 2019</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fsanz2019-25th-australian-total-diet-study">25th Australian Total Diet Study</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Standards Australia New Zealand</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fsanz2019-25th-australian-total-diet-study">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

<ul>
<li>
<strong>Metals measured:</strong>

Lead, cadmium, total arsenic, total mercury, nickel and chromium and others, in 68 results from 7 sources.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Burgon VH · 2023</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Cacau e Chocolates &quot;Bean to Bar&quot;: Contaminantes Inorgânicos e Ocratoxina A (Cocoa beans and &quot;Bean to Bar&quot; chocolates: Inorganic contaminants and Ochratoxin A)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Master&#39;s thesis, Instituto de Tecnologia de Alimentos (ITAL), Centro de Ciência e Qualidade de Alimentos, Campinas-SP, Brazil</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</li>
<li>
<strong>Main drivers:</strong>

Chocolate reduced-fat milk, as sold from all samples of type (n=3) had cadmium higher at 52 µg/kg, against 25 µg/kg for all samples of type (n=12) in the same study.<sup class="cite">[[sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Burgon VH · 2023</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Cacau e Chocolates &quot;Bean to Bar&quot;: Contaminantes Inorgânicos e Ocratoxina A (Cocoa beans and &quot;Bean to Bar&quot; chocolates: Inorganic contaminants and Ochratoxin A)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Master&#39;s thesis, Instituto de Tecnologia de Alimentos (ITAL), Centro de Ciência e Qualidade de Alimentos, Campinas-SP, Brazil</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</li>
<li>
<strong>Practical framing:</strong>

Ask for lot results with a quantification limit well below the limit that applies in the destination market.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Burgon VH · 2023</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Cacau e Chocolates &quot;Bean to Bar&quot;: Contaminantes Inorgânicos e Ocratoxina A (Cocoa beans and &quot;Bean to Bar&quot; chocolates: Inorganic contaminants and Ochratoxin A)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Master&#39;s thesis, Instituto de Tecnologia de Alimentos (ITAL), Centro de Ciência e Qualidade de Alimentos, Campinas-SP, Brazil</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</li>
</ul>
</section>

href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> In the US Total Diet Study, chocolate reduced-fat milk, as sold had a median lead result of 1.2 µg/kg across 27 composites from 2017–2020.<sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and →</a></span></span></sup>

Across 3 other studies of ordinary farms and markets, reported lead means and medians on an as-sold or fresh-weight basis ranged from 1.1 µg/kg (Spungen et al. 2024) to 28 µg/kg (Zealand 2011).<sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; chocolates: Inorganic contaminants and Ochratoxin A)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Master&#39;s thesis, Instituto de Tecnologia de Alimentos (ITAL), Centro de Ciência e Qualidade de Alimentos, Campinas-SP, Brazil</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> Higher results came from contaminated or exposed settings rather than ordinary supply. Chocolate reduced-fat milk, as sold from all samples of type (n=3) had cadmium higher at 52 µg/kg, against 25 µg/kg for all samples of type (n=12) in the same study.<sup class="cite">[[sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Burgon VH · 2023</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Cacau e Chocolates &quot;Bean to Bar&quot;: Contaminantes Inorgânicos e Ocratoxina A (Cocoa beans and &quot;Bean to Bar&quot; chocolates: Inorganic contaminants and Ochratoxin A)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Master&#39;s thesis, Instituto de Tecnologia de Alimentos (ITAL), Centro de Ciência e Qualidade de Alimentos, Campinas-SP, Brazil</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

For a buyer the practical points are the form bought, the basis a result is reported on, where the chocolate reduced-fat milk came from and the laboratory’s reporting limit. Testing finds contamination but does not remove it.



<a id="ingredient-identity-and-scope"></a>
## What counts as chocolate reduced-fat milk

<a id="hmi-ing-001"></a>
<a id="what-commodity-biological-species-edible-part-and-commercial-forms-does-this-page-cover"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-002"></a>
<a id="which-processing-states-and-derivatives-are-distinct-materials-rather-than-synonyms"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-003"></a>
<a id="what-evidence-can-be-transferred-between-those-materials-and-what-requires-a-separate-answer"></a>

Results stay on the basis each source reports: as sold or fresh weight, dry weight, or unstated. Dry-weight, as-sold and unstated results are never pooled, and no moisture conversion is applied.

<div class="hmi-analyte-explorer" id="hmi-occurrence-chocolate-reduced-fat-milk" data-analyte-explorer data-compact-intro="true" data-source-summaries="true"><details class="hmi-drawer-disclosure" data-explorer-context><summary data-open-context aria-haspopup="dialog" aria-controls="hmi-occurrence-chocolate-reduced-fat-milk-drawer">Comparison methods and scope</summary><div data-explorer-context-content>

<p>Each row is one statistic a source reports for one form of chocolate reduced-fat milk, group and metal; percentiles, ranges and maxima reported for the same samples as a mean or median are shown on that row. Different forms and preparation states are kept apart, and dry-weight results never share a reference with as-sold or fresh-weight results. Ratings place a result against an HMI reference built from monitoring results and ordinary-setting medians for the same form and basis. Same-study contrasts compare a contaminated or treated site with its control, targeted with random sampling, a prepared form with the unprocessed one, or one group of a study with the median of its other groups. Held rows failed a check against their own source and are shown but not compared.</p>

Distribution of subgroup median concentration values.</p><div class="hmi-drawer-table"><table><thead><tr><th scope="col">Reference (µg/kg)</th><th scope="col">Q1</th><th scope="col">Median</th><th scope="col">Q3</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Primary HMI reference</td><td>1.3</td><td>6</td><td>18.8</td></tr><tr><td>Alternative weighting</td><td>1.3</td><td>6</td><td>18.8</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p>29 reference units; 3 sampling families. Counts describe evidence units, not independent studies or certainty.</p><div class="hmi-drawer-table"><table><thead><tr><th scope="col">Contributing family</th><th scope="col">Reference units</th><th scope="col">Weight</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>WHO GEMS/Food</td><td>1</td><td>33%</td></tr><tr><td>FDA Total Diet Study</td><td>27</td><td>33%</td></tr><tr><td>Zealand 2019</td><td>1</td><td>33%</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Methods</p><p>HMI pools subgroup medians for chocolate reduced-fat milk, as sold on a as sold, as eaten or fresh weight basis: monitoring results summarized as one median per reporting region, sampling type and collection year, total diet composites and printed sample values as single observations, and printed subgroup medians from ordinary farms or markets. Each sampling family has equal total weight. Non-detects stay as zero-to-limit intervals, so quartiles carry lower and upper bounds. Means, ranges and maxima never enter as observations. Other forms, other bases, targeted sampling and results from mining, industrial, wastewater-affected, experimental, fertiliser or manufacturer settings are kept out of this reference.</p><p>Inverse weighted empirical CDF; midpoint at exact cumulative-weight boundaries. At least four reference units and a definitely positive Q1 to Q3 spread are required. This is a display eligibility rule, not statistical validation.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Limitations</p><p>3 sampling families with different designs; none is a probability sample of world supply. Reporting regions and purchase markets are not growing areas. Non-detects with differing limits widen the bounds. A position is descriptive concentration, not a safety or compliance judgment.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Admitted source families</p><ul><li><a href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants" class="internal" data-slug="sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants" data-no-cite-meta>WHO GEMS/Food</a></li><li><a href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020" class="internal" data-slug="sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020" data-no-cite-meta>FDA Total Diet Study</a></li><li><a href="../sources/fsanz2019-25th-australian-total-diet-study" class="internal" data-slug="sources/fsanz2019-25th-australian-total-diet-study" data-no-cite-meta>Zealand 2019</a></li></ul><details class="hmi-drawer-disclosure"><summary>Reference version and interpretation</summary><p>Frozen evidence selection: 2026-10-22. Each sampling family has equal total weight. Non-detects stay as zero-to-limit intervals, so quartiles carry lower and upper bounds. Means, ranges and maxima never enter as observations. GEMS/Food</a></p><p>Chocolate reduced-fat milk, as sold · Lead (Pb) · 8 results.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Selected finding and reference</p><div class="hmi-selected-result" data-selected-finding><strong class="hmi-finding">18.8 µg/kg (median)</strong><span class="hmi-row-note">median.</span><p>8 results. Basis: As sold, as eaten or fresh weight.</p><p>Non-detects are intervals from zero to the reporting limit; never a measured zero</p></div><div class="hmi-selected-comparison" data-selected-comparison><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">HMI baseline position</p><p>Moderate · HMI chocolate reduced-fat milk Pb reference · Chocolate reduced-fat milk, as sold. median as reported: 18.8 µg/kg. HMI median 6; Q1 1.3; Q3 18.8 µg/kg. 3 families. Not a safety grade.</p><p>Low is below Q1; Moderate includes Q1 through Q3; High is above Q3. Reporting bounds must give the same category throughout.</p><p>This finding’s sampling family contributes to the fixed reference.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Sensitivity to weighting</p><p>Equal weight per subgroup: Moderate. The position is unchanged under this alternative. This check does not address all sampling, rounding or analytical uncertainty.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Sensitivity to source selection</p><ul><li>Without WHO GEMS/Food: High.</li><li>Without FDA Total Diet Study: Limited data.</li><li>Without Zealand 2019: Moderate.</li></ul><p>These checks show dependence on each sampling family; they are not confidence intervals.</p><div data-baseline-reference="hmi-pooled-reference-v2:chocolate-reduced-fat-milk-8c060caad952:ef244402320e"><a href="#hmi-reference-hmi-pooled-reference-v2:chocolate-reduced-fat-milk-8c060caad952:ef244402320e">Reference, contributing evidence and methods</a></div></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Collection period</p><p data-timeline-scope>2009</p><div data-selected-timeline><p>No year-by-year sample register is reconstructed from this summary.</p></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Geographic scope</p><p data-regional-scope>WPRO</p><div data-selected-regional><p>Purchase or reporting market; growing area not identified.</p></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Sampling, analysis and reporting limits</p><p>As described by the monitoring programme.</p><p>Non-detects are intervals from zero to the reporting limit; never a measured zero</p><p></p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Shared samples and study overlap</p><p>Subgroup rows from the same source share its laboratory and sampling frame; they are not independent studies.</p><p>Each table row describes a food/analyte result. Repeated rows do not add independent samples or datasets. No cross-study trend is calculated in this comparison mode.</p><details class="hmi-drawer-disclosure"><summary>Reported values, conversion and source locator</summary><div class="hmi-drawer-table"><table><thead><tr><th scope="col">Source statistic</th><th scope="col">Original result</th><th scope="col">Displayed unit conversion</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>median</td><td>5 of 8 detected; HMI median of individual results</td><td>as recorded in the findings store</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p>8 dataset rows, for example Lead.csv data row 41137.</p><p>No moisture, cooking-yield or species conversion is applied to the original finding above. Any basis conversion used for its HMI comparison is stated separately with the numerical target.</p></details><p><a href="https://extranet.who.int/gemsfood/?DisplayFormat=1">Open the primary publication</a> · <a href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants" class="internal" data-slug="sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">HMI source record</a></p></div><div data-evidence-content><p class="hmi-dataset-title"><a href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020" class="internal" data-slug="sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FDA Total Diet Study</a></p><p>Chocolate reduced-fat milk, as sold · Lead (Pb) · 27 composites.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Selected finding and reference</p><div class="hmi-selected-result" data-selected-finding><strong class="hmi-finding">1.2 µg/kg (median)</strong><span class="hmi-row-note">median.</span><p>27 composites. Basis: As sold, as eaten or fresh weight.</p><p>Non-detects are intervals from zero to the reporting limit; never a measured zero</p></div><div class="hmi-selected-comparison" data-selected-comparison><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">HMI baseline position</p><p>Low · HMI chocolate reduced-fat milk Pb reference · Chocolate reduced-fat milk, as sold. median as reported: 1.2 µg/kg. <a id="sources-and-contamination-pathways"></a>
## How metals reach chocolate reduced-fat milk

<a id="hmi-ing-010"></a>
<a id="what-evidence-supports-root-uptake-surface-deposition-feed-transfer-or-other-biological-pathways"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-011"></a>
<a id="what-contamination-can-arise-from-agricultural-inputs-harvesting-processing-equipment-or-packaging"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-012"></a>
<a id="which-source-attribution-claims-are-measured-modelled-inferred-or-unresolved"></a>

The evidence on how metals reach chocolate reduced-fat milk comes from studies that compared exposed and ordinary settings. Chocolate reduced-fat milk, as sold from all samples of type (n=3) had cadmium higher at 52 µg/kg, against 25 µg/kg for all samples of type (n=12) in the same study.<sup class="cite">[[sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Burgon VH · 2023</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Cacau e Chocolates &quot;Bean to Bar&quot;: Contaminantes Inorgânicos e Ocratoxina A (Cocoa beans and &quot;Bean to Bar&quot; chocolates: Inorganic contaminants and Ochratoxin A)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Master&#39;s thesis, Instituto de Tecnologia de Alimentos (ITAL), Centro de Ciência e Qualidade de Alimentos, Campinas-SP, Brazil</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

These are observational comparisons from single studies; the size of the difference depends on the site, metal and distance.

<a id="universal-faqs"></a>
## Chocolate reduced-fat milk FAQs

<details class="qa" data-hmi-faq="1" id="what-heavy-metals-are-found-in-chocolate-reduced-fat-milk" data-anchor="what-heavy-metals-are-found-in-chocolate-reduced-fat-milk" data-question="What heavy metals are found in chocolate reduced-fat milk?">
<summary><span class="mk" aria-hidden="true"></span>What heavy metals are found in chocolate reduced-fat milk?</summary>
<div class="ans" data-hmi-faq-answer="1">

The library reports lead, cadmium, total arsenic, total mercury, nickel, chromium, aluminium, uranium, Co, Cu, Mn, Zn, Se, Ca, F and K in chocolate reduced-fat milk, most often lead, cadmium and total arsenic.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Burgon VH · 2023</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Cacau e Chocolates &quot;Bean to Bar&quot;: Contaminantes Inorgânicos e Ocratoxina A (Cocoa beans and &quot;Bean to Bar&quot; chocolates: Inorganic contaminants and Ochratoxin A)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Master&#39;s thesis, Instituto de Tecnologia de Alimentos (ITAL), Centro de Ciência e Qualidade de Alimentos, Campinas-SP, Brazil</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> WHO GEMS/Food submissions reported lead in chocolate reduced-fat milk, as sold from 9 results in 2 reporting regions, with a subgroup median of 18.8 µg/kg.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</div>
</details>

<details class="qa" data-hmi-faq="1" id="how-do-heavy-metals-get-into-chocolate-reduced-fat-milk" data-anchor="how-do-heavy-metals-get-into-chocolate-reduced-fat-milk" data-question="How do heavy metals get into chocolate reduced-fat milk?">
<summary><span class="mk" aria-hidden="true"></span>How do heavy metals get into chocolate reduced-fat milk?</summary>
<div class="ans" data-hmi-faq-answer="1">

In the studies available, chocolate reduced-fat milk from contaminated or exposed settings carried more metal than chocolate reduced-fat milk from the same study’s control.<sup class="cite">[[sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Burgon VH · 2023</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Cacau e Chocolates &quot;Bean to Bar&quot;: Contaminantes Inorgânicos e Ocratoxina A (Cocoa beans and &quot;Bean to Bar&quot; chocolates: Inorganic contaminants and Ochratoxin A)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Master&#39;s thesis, Instituto de Tecnologia de Alimentos (ITAL), Centro de Ciência e Qualidade de Alimentos, Campinas-SP, Brazil</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> Chocolate reduced-fat milk, as sold from all samples of type (n=3) had cadmium higher at 52 µg/kg, against 25 µg/kg for all samples of type (n=12) in the same study.<sup class="cite">[[sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Burgon VH · 2023</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Cacau e Chocolates &quot;Bean to Bar&quot;: Contaminantes Inorgânicos e Ocratoxina A (Cocoa beans and &quot;Bean to Bar&quot; chocolates: Inorganic contaminants and Ochratoxin A)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Master&#39;s thesis, Instituto de Tecnologia de Alimentos (ITAL), Centro de Ciência e Qualidade de Alimentos, Campinas-SP, Brazil</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/burgon2023-cocoa-bean-to-bar-brazil-metals-ota">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</div>
</details>

<details class="qa" data-hmi-faq="1" id="which-regions-report-the-lowest-levels-in-chocolate-reduced-fat-milk" data-anchor="which-regions-report-the-lowest-levels-in-chocolate-reduced-fat-milk" data-question="Which regions report the lowest levels in chocolate reduced-fat milk?">
<summary><span class="mk" aria-hidden="true"></span>Which regions report the lowest levels in chocolate reduced-fat milk?</summary>
<div class="ans" data-hmi-faq-answer="1">

Among GEMS/Food reporting regions, chocolate reduced-fat milk, as sold had the lowest cadmium subgroup median in PAHO (2 µg/kg) and the highest in PAHO (8.8 µg/kg); reporting regions are markets, not growing areas.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</div>
</details>
